The hand sections below ran on 2026-11-07 (UTC). The first four were first filed in `placement-20261106-dnd_5461_placement_rules.md` and are moved here verbatim (ADR 003: one file per domain, per branch, per day); the generated runs above are untouched.

## DND-5462 critic round 4: a module in its owner's file, a one-file directory

Group D: `mix test --timeout 2000 --max-cases 2 test/anchor/domain/placement/directories_test.exs test/anchor/domain/placement/tie_break_test.exs test/anchor/domain/graph_rules/directory_placement_test.exs test/anchor/domain/placement_test.exs test/anchor/integration/placement_invariant_test.exs test/anchor/domain/graph_report_test.exs`, through `test-slot`.

| # | Mutation | File | Result | Failing tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DIR-T | a module in its dominator's own file still moves | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/directories.ex` | `137 tests, 1 failure` (red) | proposals/3 a module defined in its dominator's own file gives nothing |
| DIR-U | a one-file directory of several modules can be flattened | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/directories.ex` | `137 tests, 1 failure` (red) | proposals/3 a directory of one file, however many modules it defines, is not inert |

### DIR-T: a module in its dominator's own file still moves

Mutation:

```elixir
# before
if current != companion and class.source_file != owner.source_file,
# after
if current != companion,
```

First failure, verbatim:

```text
  1) test proposals/3 a module defined in its dominator's own file gives nothing (Anchor.Domain.Placement.DirectoriesTest)

                level: :directory,
                subject: Rnd,
                current: "lib/b/domain",
                proposed: "lib/b/domain/invoice",

                reason: :dominator_subtree,
                evidence: ["dominated by Inv"],
                key: "directory_placement|directory|Rnd|lib/b/domain/invoice"
              }
            ]
     right: []
     stacktrace:
       test/anchor/domain/placement/directories_test.exs:91: (test)
............................................................................................................
137 tests, 1 failure
```

### DIR-U: a one-file directory of several modules can be flattened

Mutation:

```elixir
# before
with [_, _ | _] <- files,
# after
with [_, _ | _] <- modules,
         [_ | _] <- files,
```

First failure, verbatim:

```text

                level: :directory,
                subject: "lib/b/domain/util",
```

## DND-5462 critic round 5: the repeated-parameter forwarder test, restored

Group F: `mix test --timeout 2000 --max-cases 2 test/anchor/domain/placement/forwarders_test.exs test/anchor/domain/graph_rules/no_inert_hop_test.exs test/anchor/domain/placement_test.exs`, through `test-slot`.

| # | Mutation | File | Result | Failing tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HOP-X | a repeated parameter's =:= guard is accepted, and its two variables read as one | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/forwarders.ex` | `91 tests, 1 failure` (red) | forwarder/1 a repeated parameter is not a forwarder |

### HOP-X: a repeated parameter's =:= guard is accepted, and its two variables read as one

Mutation:

```elixir
# before
         pattern_vars when is_list(pattern_vars) <- vars(:cerl.clause_pats(clause)),
         true <- true_guard?(:cerl.clause_guard(clause)) do
      call_of(:cerl.clause_body(clause), pattern_vars, %{})
# after
         pattern_vars when is_list(pattern_vars) <- vars(:cerl.clause_pats(clause)),
         guard = :cerl.clause_guard(clause),
         true <-
           true_guard?(guard) or
             (:cerl.type(guard) == :call and :cerl.atom_val(:cerl.call_name(guard)) == :"=:=") do
      pair = if true_guard?(guard), do: [], else: Enum.map(:cerl.call_args(guard), &:cerl.var_name/1)
      first = Enum.find(pattern_vars, &(&1 in pair))
      call_of(:cerl.clause_body(clause), Enum.map(pattern_vars, &if(&1 in pair, do: first, else: &1)), %{})
```

## DND-5463 critic round 2: no name is given twice

Group E: `mix test --timeout 2000 --max-cases 2 test/anchor/domain/placement/module_components_test.exs test/anchor/domain/graph_rules/module_one_component_test.exs test/anchor/domain/placement_test.exs test/anchor/integration/placement_invariant_test.exs`, through `test-slot`.

MC-Y and MC-Z ran at c1d433e (101 tests). MC-AA and MC-AC first forms left a variable unused and did not compile; the rows are their second forms, at c1d433e. MC-AB's first run survived (no test reached a part's arity fallback); a945b51 adds that test, and the row is its run there (102 tests).

| # | Mutation | File | Result | Failing tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MC-Y | a name is given whether or not it is taken | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `101 tests, 4 failures` (red) | proposals/1 when every name candidate is taken, a numbered part name, never a clash; proposals/1 two parts never get one name: the second falls back to its first function; proposals/1 a part never gets the module's own name; proposals/1 a shared helper's module never takes a part's name |
| MC-Z | a part's name is not kept as taken | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `101 tests, 1 failure` (red) | proposals/1 two parts never get one name: the second falls back to its first function |
| MC-AA | the helpers do not see the parts' names | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `101 tests, 2 failures` (red) | proposals/1 a shared helper's module never takes a part's name; proposals/1 when every name candidate is taken, a numbered part name, never a clash |
| MC-AB | the arity fallback always reads arity 0 | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `102 tests, 1 failure` (red) | proposals/1 two parts led by one name at two arities: the second takes its arity |
| MC-AC | the rename note does not name the new module | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `101 tests, 1 failure` (red) | proposals/1 two parts never get one name: the second falls back to its first function |

## Review floor: lost precision, roots, a GenServer's client API

Group R: `mix test --timeout 2000 --max-cases 2 test/anchor/domain/graph_rules/directory_placement_test.exs test/anchor/domain/graph_rules/no_inert_hop_test.exs test/anchor/domain/placement/module_components_test.exs test/anchor/domain/failures_graph_test.exs test/anchor/integration/placement_invariant_test.exs test/anchor/domain/placement/forwarders_test.exs`, through `test-slot`.

RF-A to RF-E ran at ebb959b (97 tests): each reverts one review-floor fix.

| # | Mutation | File | Result | Failing tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RF-A | lost precision is judged as precise | `lib/anchor/domain/graph_rules/directory_placement.ex` | `97 tests, 3 failures` (red) | lost precision: one fail-closed issue naming the site, and no finding; spawn with a computed name loses reachability, naming its line; the precision is printed, and a computed name turns reachability into one issue |
| RF-B | a root is judged as a forwarder | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/forwarders.ex` | `97 tests, 1 failure` (red) | a root that forwards is never inlined: its callers are outside the project |
| RF-C | a private function's call into the behaviour does not link | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `97 tests, 1 failure` (red) | components/2 a GenServer's client API is one component with its callbacks |
| RF-D | a public function's own call into the behaviour does not link | `lib/anchor/domain/placement/module_components.ex` | `97 tests, 1 failure` (red) | components/2 a GenServer's client API is one component with its callbacks |
| RF-E | the lost-precision Fix always names reachable_from_root | `lib/anchor/domain/failures.ex` | `97 tests, 1 failure` (red) | lost precision: one fail-closed issue naming the site, and no finding |

### RF-A: lost precision is judged as precise

Mutation:

```elixir
# before
      :lost ->
        [Failures.reachability_lost(rule, placement.precision.lost_sites, model.config.path)]
# after
      :lost ->
        judged(placement, model, rule)
```
